- **funnel-conversion-analysis**: Use for stage-to-stage conversion, funnel drop-off, lead-to-opportunity or MQL-to-close analysis, and for comparing funnel performance across segments, sources or time. - **fuse-elementwise-ops**: 2. **Count memory traffic before and after fusion.** For an unfused chain of N operations over a tensor of size S bytes: - Unfused traffic = 2 * N * S (N reads + N writes, ignoring L2 reuse). - Fused traffic = 2 * S (one read pass, one write pass). - Estimate bandwidth saving as (2N - 2) * S.
